province_event = {
	id = 148001
	title = "0"
	desc = "0"
	trigger = {
		is_core = ZUL
		NOT = { culture = zulu }
		ZUL = { exists = no }
		any_neighbor_province = {
			NOT = { is_core = ZUL }
		}
		owner = { AI = yes }
		NOT = { has_province_flag = ZUL_core_remove }
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 12
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = {
			which = core_counter
			value = 0
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = ZUL
			}
			set_province_flag = counted
			change_variable = {
				which = core_counter
				value = 1
			}
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = ZUL
				NOT = { has_province_flag = counted }
			}
			set_province_flag = counted
			change_variable = {
				which = core_counter
				value = 1
			}
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = ZUL
				NOT = { has_province_flag = counted }
			}
			change_variable = {
				which = core_counter
				value = 1
			}
		}
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = counted
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "1"
		set_province_flag = ZUL_core_remove
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						which = core_counter
						value = 3
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "2"
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				check_variable = {
					which = core_counter
					value = 3
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
